- **gpu-onchain-deregister**: Deactivate an on-chain GPU registration (signed by its registrant). The registration stays on the chain with status 'deactivated' and takes no new allocations; registering the same gpu-id again reactivates it. Takes effect only once the chain has activated state-transition v10; before that the node refuses the transaction. This is not `aitbc gpu unregister`, which only edits the local GPU service. ([oib/AITBC](https://github.com/oib/AITBC/blob/HEAD/docs/cli/commands/gpu-onchain-deregister.md))

Dispatches the `devils-advocate` adversary (which resolves a three-ring codebase blast radius and self-gated web-verification), cross-examines every attack with a non-author refutation pass (architect excluded), writes `specs/[feature]/grill.md`, and recommends a 4-way disposition (PROCEED / REVISE-PLAN / RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM / KILL). Opt-in — never an auto-gate. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))

Triggers: "grill my plan", "stress-test this plan", "validate this approach", "before I implement X", "is this consistent with our domain", "challenge my assumptions".
